- [ATRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14627): Reduces multilingual audio-text retrieval inconsistency by modeling distribution-error effects in cross-modal alignment.

- [DualSpec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18952): Generates spatial audio from text using dual-spectrogram guided diffusion.

- [Late Fusion and Multi-Level Fission Amplify Cross-Modal Transfer in Text-Speech LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06211): Replaces early text-speech fusion with late fusion and multi-level fission so SmolTolk can share a hierarchy across text and finer-grained speech features with far less compute.
